# 1ST_READ.BIN's heat: a measurement of how often a frame ran each stretch
# of code, by which tools/order.py costs the places of kernel.txt's warm
# rows. It holds frames spent standing at Emerald Coast's start, at Windy
# Valley's four rings and in Sky Deck's third act, at 60 frames a second:
# a column of RUNS for each route, in that order.
#
# frames FRAMES... gives the frames measured on each route. code IMAGE
# PATH INPUT opens the stretches of INPUT, an input section of IMAGE's
# object PATH, as ld's map names it, and fixed those of code no image
# links. A stretch is OFFSET SIZE RUNS...: SIZE bytes of instructions from
# OFFSET bytes into the section, or from the address OFFSET, each of which
# a route's frames ran RUNS times. Where a function was entered, or
# returned into from a call, less often than one of its instructions ran,
# as one that loops, the stretch gives those visits, since a visit loads a
# line once. A stretch keeps its place in its section through a link that
# moves the section, so the heat serves later links until the code that is
# hot changes: make dc-order says how many stretches the link still holds.
frames  330  270  270
